    179             1                    ***0***                 tx.row_a_in_flat[((i*MAC_PER_PE + 3)*DATA_WIDTH) +: DATA_WIDTH] = 8'd100;
    180                                              
    181             1                    ***0***                 tx.col_b_in_flat[((i*MAC_PER_PE + 0)*DATA_WIDTH) +: DATA_WIDTH] = 8'd9;
    182             1                    ***0***                 tx.col_b_in_flat[((i*MAC_PER_PE + 1)*DATA_WIDTH) +: DATA_WIDTH] = 8'd15;
    183             1                    ***0***                 tx.col_b_in_flat[((i*MAC_PER_PE + 2)*DATA_WIDTH) +: DATA_WIDTH] = 8'd12;
    184             1                    ***0***                 tx.col_b_in_flat[((i*MAC_PER_PE + 3)*DATA_WIDTH) +: DATA_WIDTH] = 8'd3;
    185                                                      end
    186                                              
    187             1                    ***0***             tx.inject_fault  = 1'b1;
    188             1                    ***0***             tx.fault_pe_id   = 6'd63;  // PE[7][7]
    189             1                    ***0***             tx.fault_bit_pos = 6'd10;  // flip bit 10
    190             1                    ***0***             tx.fault_cycle   = 10;
    191                                              
    192             1                    ***0***             finish_item(tx);
    193                                              	    endtask
    194                                              
    195                                              	endclass
    196                                              
    197                                              	class accel_random_fault_seq extends uvm_sequence #(accel_txn);
    198                                              
    199             1                    ***0***     	    `uvm_object_utils(accel_random_fault_seq)
    199             2                    ***0***     
    199             3                    ***0***     
    199             4                    ***0***     
    199             5                    ***0***     
    199             6                    ***0***     
    199             7                    ***0***     
    199             8                    ***0***     
    199             9                    ***0***     
    199            10                    ***0***     
    200                                              
    201                                               	   function new(string name = "accel_random_fault_seq");
    202             1                    ***0***      	       super.new(name);
    203                                               	   endfunction
    204                                              
    205                                               	   virtual task body();
    206                                               	       accel_txn tx;
    207                                              
    208             1                    ***0***       	      repeat (10) begin
    209             1                    ***0***     	            tx = accel_txn::type_id::create("tx_random_fault");
    210                                              
    211             1                    ***0***                 start_item(tx);
    212                                              
    213             1                    ***0***                 for (int i = 0; i < N; i++) begin
    213             2                    ***0***     
    214             1                    ***0***                     tx.row_a_in_flat[((i*MAC_PER_PE + 0)*DATA_WIDTH) +: DATA_WIDTH] = 8'd7;
    215             1                    ***0***                     tx.row_a_in_flat[((i*MAC_PER_PE + 1)*DATA_WIDTH) +: DATA_WIDTH] = 8'd15;
    216             1                    ***0***                     tx.row_a_in_flat[((i*MAC_PER_PE + 2)*DATA_WIDTH) +: DATA_WIDTH] = 8'd25;
    217             1                    ***0***                     tx.row_a_in_flat[((i*MAC_PER_PE + 3)*DATA_WIDTH) +: DATA_WIDTH] = 8'd100;
    218                                              
    219             1                    ***0***                     tx.col_b_in_flat[((i*MAC_PER_PE + 0)*DATA_WIDTH) +: DATA_WIDTH] = 8'd9;
    220             1                    ***0***                     tx.col_b_in_flat[((i*MAC_PER_PE + 1)*DATA_WIDTH) +: DATA_WIDTH] = 8'd15;
    221             1                    ***0***                     tx.col_b_in_flat[((i*MAC_PER_PE + 2)*DATA_WIDTH) +: DATA_WIDTH] = 8'd12;
    222             1                    ***0***                     tx.col_b_in_flat[((i*MAC_PER_PE + 3)*DATA_WIDTH) +: DATA_WIDTH] = 8'd3;
    223                                                          end
    224                                              
    225             1                    ***0***                 tx.inject_fault  = 1'b1;
    226             1                    ***0***                 tx.fault_pe_id   = $urandom_range(0, 63);
    227             1                    ***0***                 tx.fault_bit_pos = $urandom_range(0, 33);
    228             1                    ***0***                 tx.fault_cycle   = $urandom_range(5, 20);
    229                                              
    230             1                    ***0***                 finish_item(tx);
    231                                              	        end
    232                                              	    endtask
    233                                              
    234                                              	endclass
    235                                              
    236

    251                                                  // ============================================================
    252                                                  // 5. Driver
    253                                                  // ============================================================
    254                                                  class accel_driver extends uvm_driver #(accel_txn);
    255                                              
    256             1                    ***0***             `uvm_component_utils(accel_driver)
    256             2                    ***0***     
    256             3                    ***0***     
    257                                              
    258                                                      virtual accel_if.drv_mp vif;
    259                                              
    260                                                      function new(string name = "accel_driver", uvm_component parent = null);
    261             1                          1                 super.new(name, parent);
    262                                                      endfunction
    263                                              
    264                                                      function void build_phase(uvm_phase phase);
    265             1                          1                 super.build_phase(phase);
    266                                              
    267                                                          if (!uvm_config_db#(virtual accel_if.drv_mp)::get(this, "", "vif", vif)) begin
    268             1                    ***0***                     `uvm_fatal("NOVIF", "Driver virtual interface not found")
    269                                                          end
    270                                                      endfunction
    271                                              
    272                                                     task reset_dut();
    273             1                          1     	    vif.rst_n         <= 1'b0;
    274             1                          1     	    vif.start         <= 1'b0;
    275             1                          1     	    vif.clear_acc     <= 1'b0;
    276             1                          1     	    vif.row_a_in_flat <= '0;
    277             1                          1     	    vif.col_b_in_flat <= '0;
    278                                              
    279             1                          1     	    vif.fault_en      <= 1'b0;
    280             1                          1     	    vif.fault_pe_id   <= 6'd0;
    281             1                          1     	    vif.fault_bit_pos <= 6'd0;
    282                                              
    283             1                          1     	    vif.fault_was_injected      <= 1'b0;
    284             1                          1     	    vif.injected_fault_pe_id    <= 6'd0;
    285             1                          1     	    vif.injected_fault_bit_pos  <= 6'd0;
    286                                              
    287             1                          5     	    repeat (5) @(posedge vif.clk);
    287             2                          5     
    288                                              
    289             1                          1     	    vif.rst_n <= 1'b1;
    290                                              
    291             1                          2     	    repeat (2) @(posedge vif.clk);
    291             2                          2     
    292                                              
    293             1                          1     	    vif.clear_acc <= 1'b1;
    294             1                          1     	    @(posedge vif.clk);
    295             1                          1     	    vif.clear_acc <= 1'b0;
    296                                              
    297             1                          1     	    `uvm_info("DRV", "DUT reset completed", UVM_LOW)
    298                                              	endtask
    299                                              
    300                                                    task drive_one_txn(accel_txn tx);
    301                                                  int cycle;
    302                                              
    303             1                          1         @(posedge vif.clk);
    304                                              
    305             1                          1         vif.row_a_in_flat <= tx.row_a_in_flat;
    306             1                          1         vif.col_b_in_flat <= tx.col_b_in_flat;
    307                                              
    308             1                          1         vif.fault_en      <= 1'b0;
    309             1                          1         vif.fault_pe_id   <= tx.fault_pe_id;
    310             1                          1         vif.fault_bit_pos <= tx.fault_bit_pos;
    311                                              
    312             1                          1         vif.fault_was_injected      <= 1'b0;
    313             1                          1         vif.injected_fault_pe_id    <= tx.fault_pe_id;
    314             1                          1         vif.injected_fault_bit_pos  <= tx.fault_bit_pos;
    315                                              
    316             1                          1         @(posedge vif.clk);
    317             1                          1         vif.start <= 1'b1;
    318                                              
    319             1                          1         @(posedge vif.clk);
    320             1                          1         vif.start <= 1'b0;
    321                                              
    322             1                          1         `uvm_info("DRV",
    323                                                      $sformatf("Transaction started. inject_fault=%0b pe_id=%0d bit=%0d cycle=%0d",
    324                                                                tx.inject_fault, tx.fault_pe_id, tx.fault_bit_pos, tx.fault_cycle),
    325                                                      UVM_MEDIUM)
    326                                              
    327             1                          1         cycle = 0;
    328                                              
    329             1                         18         while (vif.done !== 1'b1) begin
    330             1                         17             @(posedge vif.clk);
    331             1                         17             cycle++;
    332                                              
    333                                                      if (tx.inject_fault && cycle == tx.fault_cycle) begin
    334             1                          1                 vif.fault_en      <= 1'b1;
    335             1                          1                 vif.fault_pe_id   <= tx.fault_pe_id;
    336             1                          1                 vif.fault_bit_pos <= tx.fault_bit_pos;
    337                                              
    338             1                          1                 vif.fault_was_injected      <= 1'b1;
    339             1                          1                 vif.injected_fault_pe_id    <= tx.fault_pe_id;
    340             1                          1                 vif.injected_fault_bit_pos  <= tx.fault_bit_pos;
    341                                              
    342             1                          1                 `uvm_info("FAULT_DRV",
    343                                                              $sformatf("Injected fault: PE_ID=%0d BIT=%0d at cycle=%0d",
    344                                                                        tx.fault_pe_id, tx.fault_bit_pos, cycle),
    345                                                              UVM_LOW)
    346                                                      end else begin
    347             1                         16                 vif.fault_en <= 1'b0;
    348                                                      end
    349                                                  end
    350                                              
    351             1                          1         vif.fault_en <= 1'b0;
    352                                              
    353             1                          1         @(posedge vif.clk);
    354                                              endtask
    355                                              
    356                                                      virtual task run_phase(uvm_phase phase);
    357                                                          accel_txn tx;
    358                                              
    359             1                          1                 reset_dut();
    360                                              
    361             1                          1                 forever begin
    362             1                          2                     seq_item_port.get_next_item(tx);
    363             1                          1                     drive_one_txn(tx);
    364             1                          1                     seq_item_port.item_done();
    365                                                          end
    366                                                      endtask
    367                                              
    368                                                  endclass
    369                                              
    370                                              
    371                                                  // ============================================================
    372                                                  // 6. Monitor
    373                                                  // ============================================================
    374                                                  class accel_monitor extends uvm_component;
    375                                              
    376             1                    ***0***             `uvm_component_utils(accel_monitor)
    376             2                    ***0***     
    376             3                    ***0***     
    377                                              
    378                                                      virtual accel_if.mon_mp vif;
    379                                                      uvm_analysis_port #(accel_txn) mon_ap;
    380                                                  // ============================================================
    381                                                  // Functional coverage variables
    382                                                  // ============================================================
    383                                                  bit        cov_inject_fault;
    384                                                  bit [5:0]  cov_fault_pe_id;
    385                                                  bit [5:0]  cov_fault_bit_pos;
    386                                                  bit [7:0]  cov_fault_cycle;
    387                                                  bit [7:0]  cov_cycle_count;
    388                                                  bit        cov_done;
    389                                                  bit        cov_result_valid;
    390                                              
    391                                                  bit [7:0]  cov_a0;
    392                                                  bit [7:0]  cov_a1;
    393                                                  bit [7:0]  cov_a2;
    394                                                  bit [7:0]  cov_a3;
    395                                              
    396                                                  bit [7:0]  cov_b0;
    397                                                  bit [7:0]  cov_b1;
    398                                                  bit [7:0]  cov_b2;
    399                                                  bit [7:0]  cov_b3;
    400                                              
    401                                                  bit [33:0] cov_pe0_sum;
    402                                                  bit [33:0] cov_pe_last_sum;
    403                                              
    404                                                  // ============================================================
    405                                                  // Functional covergroup
    406                                                  // ============================================================
    407                                                  covergroup accel_functional_cg;
    408                                                      option.per_instance = 1;
    409                                                      option.name = "accel_functional_cg";
    410                                              
    411                                                      // --------------------------------------------------------
    412                                                      // Basic protocol coverage
    413                                                      // --------------------------------------------------------
    414                                                      cp_done: coverpoint cov_done {
    415                                                          bins done_seen = {1};
    416                                                      }
    417                                              
    418                                                      cp_result_valid: coverpoint cov_result_valid {
    419                                                          bins result_valid_seen = {1};
    420                                                      }
    421                                              
    422                                                      cross_done_result_valid: cross cp_done, cp_result_valid;
    423                                              
    424                                                      // --------------------------------------------------------
    425                                                      // Fault enable coverage
    426                                                      // --------------------------------------------------------
    427                                                      cp_inject_fault: coverpoint cov_inject_fault {
    428                                                          bins no_fault = {0};
    429                                                          bins fault    = {1};
    430                                                      }
    431                                              
    432                                                      // --------------------------------------------------------
    433                                                      // Fault PE location coverage
    434                                                      // 8x8 array = 64 PEs
    435                                                      // --------------------------------------------------------
    436                                                      cp_fault_pe_id: coverpoint cov_fault_pe_id {
    437                                                          bins pe_0       = {0};
    438                                                          bins pe_low     = {[1:15]};
    439                                                          bins pe_mid     = {[16:47]};
    440                                                          bins pe_high    = {[48:62]};
    441                                                          bins pe_63      = {63};
    442                                                      }
    443                                              
    444                                                      // --------------------------------------------------------
    445                                                      // Fault bit position coverage
    446                                                      // PE output width = 34 bits, valid bits 0 to 33
    447                                                      // --------------------------------------------------------
    448                                                      cp_fault_bit_pos: coverpoint cov_fault_bit_pos {
    449                                                          bins bit_0       = {0};
    450                                                          bins low_bits    = {[1:7]};
    451                                                          bins mid_bits    = {[8:23]};
    452                                                          bins high_bits   = {[24:32]};
    453                                                          bins bit_33      = {33};
    454                                                          bins invalid_bit = {[34:63]};
    455                                                      }
    456                                              
    457                                                      // --------------------------------------------------------
    458                                                      // Fault cycle coverage
    459                                                      // --------------------------------------------------------
    460                                                      cp_fault_cycle: coverpoint cov_fault_cycle {
    461                                                          bins early_cycle = {[0:7]};
    462                                                          bins mid_cycle   = {[8:15]};
    463                                                          bins late_cycle  = {[16:31]};
    464                                                          bins very_late   = {[32:255]};
    465                                                      }
    466                                              
    467                                                      // --------------------------------------------------------
    468                                                      // Output cycle count coverage
    469                                                      // --------------------------------------------------------
    470                                                      cp_cycle_count: coverpoint cov_cycle_count {
    471                                                          bins low_count  = {[0:10]};
    472                                                          bins mid_count  = {[11:20]};
    473                                                          bins high_count = {[21:40]};
    474                                                      }
    475                                              
    476                                                      // --------------------------------------------------------
    477                                                      // Image / CNN activation input coverage
    478                                                      // --------------------------------------------------------
    479                                                      cp_a0: coverpoint cov_a0 {
    480                                                          bins zero      = {0};
    481                                                          bins low_val   = {[1:63]};
    482                                                          bins mid_val   = {[64:191]};
    483                                                          bins high_val  = {[192:254]};
    484                                                          bins max_val   = {255};
    485                                                      }
    486                                              
    487                                                      cp_a1: coverpoint cov_a1 {
    488                                                          bins zero      = {0};
    489                                                          bins low_val   = {[1:63]};
    490                                                          bins mid_val   = {[64:191]};
    491                                                          bins high_val  = {[192:254]};
    492                                                          bins max_val   = {255};
    493                                                      }
    494                                              
    495                                                      cp_a2: coverpoint cov_a2 {
    496                                                          bins zero      = {0};
    497                                                          bins low_val   = {[1:63]};
    498                                                          bins mid_val   = {[64:191]};
    499                                                          bins high_val  = {[192:254]};
    500                                                          bins max_val   = {255};
    501                                                      }
    502                                              
    503                                                      cp_a3: coverpoint cov_a3 {
    504                                                          bins zero      = {0};
    505                                                          bins low_val   = {[1:63]};
    506                                                          bins mid_val   = {[64:191]};
    507                                                          bins high_val  = {[192:254]};
    508                                                          bins max_val   = {255};
    509                                                      }
    510                                              
    511                                                      // --------------------------------------------------------
    512                                                      // CNN weight/kernel input coverage
    513                                                      // --------------------------------------------------------
    514                                                      cp_b0: coverpoint cov_b0 {
    515                                                          bins zero      = {0};
    516                                                          bins low_val   = {[1:63]};
    517                                                          bins mid_val   = {[64:191]};
    518                                                          bins high_val  = {[192:254]};
    519                                                          bins max_val   = {255};
    520                                                      }
    521                                              
    522                                                      cp_b1: coverpoint cov_b1 {
    523                                                          bins zero      = {0};
    524                                                          bins low_val   = {[1:63]};
    525                                                          bins mid_val   = {[64:191]};
    526                                                          bins high_val  = {[192:254]};
    527                                                          bins max_val   = {255};
    528                                                      }
    529                                              
    530                                                      cp_b2: coverpoint cov_b2 {
    531                                                          bins zero      = {0};
    532                                                          bins low_val   = {[1:63]};
    533                                                          bins mid_val   = {[64:191]};
    534                                                          bins high_val  = {[192:254]};
    535                                                          bins max_val   = {255};
    536                                                      }
    537                                              
    538                                                      cp_b3: coverpoint cov_b3 {
    539                                                          bins zero      = {0};
    540                                                          bins low_val   = {[1:63]};
    541                                                          bins mid_val   = {[64:191]};
    542                                                          bins high_val  = {[192:254]};
    543                                                          bins max_val   = {255};
    544                                                      }
    545                                              
    546                                                      // --------------------------------------------------------
    547                                                      // Output magnitude coverage
    548                                                      // --------------------------------------------------------
    549                                                      cp_pe0_sum: coverpoint cov_pe0_sum {
    550                                                          bins zero_out  = {0};
    551                                                          bins small_out = {[1:1000]};
    552                                                          bins mid_out   = {[1001:50000]};
    553                                                          bins high_out  = {[50001:200000]};
    554                                                          bins very_high = {[200001:$]};
    555                                                      }
    556                                              
    557                                                      cp_pe_last_sum: coverpoint cov_pe_last_sum {
    558                                                          bins zero_out  = {0};
    559                                                          bins small_out = {[1:1000]};
    560                                                          bins mid_out   = {[1001:50000]};
    561                                                          bins high_out  = {[50001:200000]};
    562                                                          bins very_high = {[200001:$]};
    563                                                      }
    564                                              
    565                                                      // --------------------------------------------------------
    566                                                      // Cross coverage
    567                                                      // --------------------------------------------------------
    568                                                      cross_fault_location: cross cp_fault_pe_id, cp_fault_bit_pos;
    569                                              
    570                                                      cross_fault_time: cross cp_fault_pe_id, cp_fault_cycle;
    571                                              
    572                                                      cross_fault_enable_location: cross cp_inject_fault, cp_fault_pe_id;
    573                                              
    574                                                      cross_input_activity: cross cp_a0, cp_b0;
    575                                              
    576                                                  endgroup
    577                                              
    578                                                      function new(string name = "accel_monitor", uvm_component parent = null);
    579             1                          1                 super.new(name, parent);
    580             1                          1                 mon_ap = new("mon_ap", this);
    581                                                      endfunction
    582                                              
    583                                                      function void build_phase(uvm_phase phase);
    584             1                          1                 super.build_phase(phase);
    585                                              
    586                                                          if (!uvm_config_db#(virtual accel_if.mon_mp)::get(this, "", "vif", vif)) begin
    587             1                    ***0***                     `uvm_fatal("NOVIF", "Monitor virtual interface not found")
    588                                                          end
    589                                                      endfunction
    590                                              
    591                                                      virtual task run_phase(uvm_phase phase);
    592                                              	    accel_txn tx;
    593                                              
    594             1                          1     	    forever begin
    595             1                         80     	        @(posedge vif.clk);
    596                                              
    597                                              	        if (vif.done && vif.result_valid) begin
    598             1                          1     	    tx = accel_txn::type_id::create("tx_mon");
    599                                              
    600             1                          1     	    tx.row_a_in_flat = vif.row_a_in_flat;
    601             1                          1     	    tx.col_b_in_flat = vif.col_b_in_flat;
    602             1                          1     	    tx.pe_sum_flat   = vif.pe_sum_flat;
    603             1                          1     	    tx.pe_valid_flat = vif.pe_valid_flat;
    604             1                          1     	    tx.done          = vif.done;
    605             1                          1     	    tx.result_valid  = vif.result_valid;
    606             1                          1     	    tx.cycle_count   = vif.cycle_count;
    607                                              
    608             1                          1     	    tx.inject_fault  = vif.fault_was_injected;
    609             1                          1     	    tx.fault_pe_id   = vif.injected_fault_pe_id;
    610             1                          1     	    tx.fault_bit_pos = vif.injected_fault_bit_pos;
    611             1                          1     	    tx.fault_cycle   = vif.cycle_count;
    612                                              
    613             1                          1     	    `uvm_info("MON", "Captured DUT output transaction", UVM_MEDIUM)
    614                                              	
    615             1                          1     	    mon_ap.write(tx);
    616                                              		end
    617                                              	    end
    618                                              	endtask
    619                                                  endclass
    620                                              
    621                                              
    622                                              // ============================================================
    623                                              // 7. Scoreboard
    624                                              // ============================================================
    625                                              class accel_scoreboard extends uvm_component;
    626                                              
    627             1                    ***0***         `uvm_component_utils(accel_scoreboard)
    627             2                    ***0***     
    627             3                    ***0***     
    628                                              
    629                                                  uvm_analysis_imp #(accel_txn, accel_scoreboard) sb_imp;
    630                                              
    631                                                  int pass_count;
    632                                                  int fail_count;
    633                                                  int mismatch_count;
    634                                              
    635                                                  longint unsigned total_error;
    636                                                  longint unsigned max_error;
    637                                                  longint unsigned local_error;
    638                                              
    639                                                  int injected_fault_count;
    640                                                  int detected_fault_count;
    641                                              
    642                                                  real fault_coverage;
    643                                                  real error_per_fault;
    644                                              
    645                                                  function new(string name = "accel_scoreboard", uvm_component parent = null);
    646             1                          1             super.new(name, parent);
    647             1                          1             sb_imp = new("sb_imp", this);
    648                                              
    649             1                          1             pass_count           = 0;
    650             1                          1             fail_count           = 0;
    651             1                          1             mismatch_count       = 0;
    652             1                          1             total_error          = 0;
    653             1                          1             max_error            = 0;
    654             1                          1             local_error          = 0;
    655             1                          1             injected_fault_count = 0;
    656             1                          1             detected_fault_count = 0;
    657             1                          1             fault_coverage       = 0.0;
    658             1                          1             error_per_fault      = 0.0;
    659                                                  endfunction
    660                                              
    661                                              
    662                                                  function bit [PE_SUM_WIDTH-1:0] get_pe_sum(
    663                                                      input bit [OUT_WIDTH-1:0] flat,
    664                                                      input int row,
    665                                                      input int col
    666                                                  );
    667             1                         64             get_pe_sum = flat[((row*N + col)*PE_SUM_WIDTH) +: PE_SUM_WIDTH];
    668                                                  endfunction
    669                                              
    670                                              
    671                                                 function bit [15:0] approx_mult(
    672                                                  input bit [7:0] a,
    673                                                  input bit [7:0] b
    674                                              );
    675                                                  bit [15:0] exact;
    676                                                  bit [15:0] approx;
    677                                                  bit [15:0] compensation;
    678                                              
    679                                                  begin
    680             1                        256             exact = a * b;
    681                                              
    682                                                      // Truncate lower bits
    683             1                        256             approx = exact;
    684             1                        256             approx[APPROX_BITS-1:0] = '0;
    685                                              
    686                                                      // Match RTL compensation:
    687                                                      // APPROX_BITS = 4 gives compensation = 8
    688             1                        256             compensation = 16'd1 << (APPROX_BITS - 1);
    689                                              
    690             1                        256             approx = approx + compensation;
    691                                              
    692             1                        256             return approx;
    693                                                  end
    694                                              endfunction
    695                                              
    696                                              
    697                                                  function bit [PE_SUM_WIDTH-1:0] expected_pe_value(
    698                                                      input bit [IN_WIDTH-1:0] row_a,
    699                                                      input bit [IN_WIDTH-1:0] col_b,
    700                                                      input int row,
    701                                                      input int col
    702                                                  );
    703                                                      bit [7:0] a0, a1, a2, a3;
    704                                                      bit [7:0] b0, b1, b2, b3;
    705                                                      bit [PE_SUM_WIDTH-1:0] exp;
    706                                              
    707                                                      begin
    708             1                         64                 a0 = row_a[((row*MAC_PER_PE + 0)*DATA_WIDTH) +: DATA_WIDTH];
    709             1                         64                 a1 = row_a[((row*MAC_PER_PE + 1)*DATA_WIDTH) +: DATA_WIDTH];
    710             1                         64                 a2 = row_a[((row*MAC_PER_PE + 2)*DATA_WIDTH) +: DATA_WIDTH];
    711             1                         64                 a3 = row_a[((row*MAC_PER_PE + 3)*DATA_WIDTH) +: DATA_WIDTH];
    712                                              
    713             1                         64                 b0 = col_b[((col*MAC_PER_PE + 0)*DATA_WIDTH) +: DATA_WIDTH];
    714             1                         64                 b1 = col_b[((col*MAC_PER_PE + 1)*DATA_WIDTH) +: DATA_WIDTH];
    715             1                         64                 b2 = col_b[((col*MAC_PER_PE + 2)*DATA_WIDTH) +: DATA_WIDTH];
    716             1                         64                 b3 = col_b[((col*MAC_PER_PE + 3)*DATA_WIDTH) +: DATA_WIDTH];
    717                                              
    718             1                         64                 exp = approx_mult(a0, b0)
    719                                                              + approx_mult(a1, b1)
    720                                                              + approx_mult(a2, b2)
    721                                                              + approx_mult(a3, b3);
    722                                              
    723             1                         64                 return exp;
    724                                                      end
    725                                                  endfunction
    726                                              
    727                                              
    728                                                  function void write(accel_txn tx);
    729                                                  bit [PE_SUM_WIDTH-1:0] actual;
    730                                                  bit [PE_SUM_WIDTH-1:0] expected;
    731                                                  bit fault_detected_this_txn;
    732                                              
    733             1                          1         `uvm_info("SB", "Scoreboard received transaction", UVM_LOW)
    734                                              
    735             1                          1         fault_detected_this_txn = 1'b0;
    736                                              
    737                                                  if (tx.done !== 1'b1 || tx.result_valid !== 1'b1) begin
    738             1                    ***0***             `uvm_error("SB", "done/result_valid protocol failure")
    739             1                    ***0***             fail_count++;
    740             1                    ***0***             return;
    741                                                  end
    742                                              
    743                                                  if (&tx.pe_valid_flat !== 1'b1) begin
    744             1                    ***0***             `uvm_error("SB", "Not all PE valid bits are high")
    745             1                    ***0***             fail_count++;
    746                                                  end
    747                                              
    748                                                  if (tx.inject_fault) begin
    749             1                          1             injected_fault_count++;
    750                                                  end
    751                                              
    752             1                          1         for (int r = 0; r < N; r++) begin
    752             2                          8     
    753             1                          8             for (int c = 0; c < N; c++) begin
    753             2                         64     
    754                                              
    755             1                         64                 actual   = get_pe_sum(tx.pe_sum_flat, r, c);
    756             1                         64                 expected = expected_pe_value(tx.row_a_in_flat, tx.col_b_in_flat, r, c);
    757                                              
    758                                                          if (actual === expected) begin
    759             1                    ***0***                     pass_count++;
    760                                                          end else begin
    761             1                         64                     mismatch_count++;
    762             1                         64                     fault_detected_this_txn = 1'b1;
    763                                              
    764                                                              if (actual > expected)
    765             1                          1                         local_error = actual - expected;
    766                                                              else
    767             1                         63                         local_error = expected - actual;
    768                                              
    769             1                         64                     total_error += local_error;
    770                                              
    771                                                              if (local_error > max_error)
    772             1                          1                         max_error = local_error;
    773                                              
    774                                                              if (tx.inject_fault) begin
    775             1                         64                         `uvm_info("SB_FAULT",
    776                                                                      $sformatf("Expected fault effect PE[%0d][%0d]: actual=%0d expected=%0d error=%0d",
    777                                                                                r, c, actual, expected, local_error),
    778                                                                      UVM_LOW)
    779                                                              end else begin
    780             1                    ***0***                         fail_count++;
    781                                              
    782             1                    ***0***                         `uvm_error("SB",
    783                                                                      $sformatf("Unexpected mismatch PE[%0d][%0d]: actual=%0d expected=%0d error=%0d",
    784                                                                                r, c, actual, expected, local_error))
    785                                                              end
    786                                                          end
    787                                                      end
    788                                                  end
    789                                              
    790                                                  if (tx.inject_fault && fault_detected_this_txn) begin
    791             1                          1             detected_fault_count++;
    792                                                  end
    793                                              
    794                                                  if (injected_fault_count > 0) begin
    795             1                          1             fault_coverage  = (detected_fault_count * 100.0) / injected_fault_count;
    796             1                          1             error_per_fault = total_error * 1.0 / injected_fault_count;
    797                                                  end else begin
    798             1                    ***0***             fault_coverage  = 0.0;
    799             1                    ***0***             error_per_fault = 0.0;
    800                                                  end
    801                                              
    802             1                          1         `uvm_info("SB",
    803                                                      $sformatf("PASS=%0d FAIL=%0d MISMATCH=%0d TOTAL_ERROR=%0d MAX_ERROR=%0d",
    804                                                                pass_count, fail_count, mismatch_count, total_error, max_error),
    805                                                      UVM_LOW)
    806                                              
    807                                              endfunction
    808                                              
    809                                              
    810                                                  function void report_phase(uvm_phase phase);
    811             1                          1             super.report_phase(phase);
    812                                              
    813             1                          1             `uvm_info("FINAL_REPORT", "=================================================", UVM_NONE)
    814             1                          1             `uvm_info("FINAL_REPORT", "Final Result Analysis Report", UVM_NONE)
    815             1                          1             `uvm_info("FINAL_REPORT", "=================================================", UVM_NONE)
    816                                              
    817             1                          1             `uvm_info("FINAL_REPORT",
    818                                                          $sformatf("PASS count           = %0d", pass_count),
    819                                                          UVM_NONE)
    820                                              
    821             1                          1             `uvm_info("FINAL_REPORT",
    822                                                          $sformatf("FAIL count           = %0d", fail_count),
    823                                                          UVM_NONE)
    824                                              
    825             1                          1             `uvm_info("FINAL_REPORT",
    826                                                          $sformatf("Mismatch count       = %0d", mismatch_count),
    827                                                          UVM_NONE)
    828                                              
    829             1                          1             `uvm_info("FINAL_REPORT",
    830                                                          $sformatf("Total error          = %0d", total_error),
    831                                                          UVM_NONE)
    832                                              
    833             1                          1             `uvm_info("FINAL_REPORT",
    834                                                          $sformatf("Maximum PE error     = %0d", max_error),
    835                                                          UVM_NONE)
    836                                              
    837             1                          1             `uvm_info("FINAL_REPORT",
    838                                                          $sformatf("Injected faults      = %0d", injected_fault_count),
    839                                                          UVM_NONE)
    840                                              
    841             1                          1             `uvm_info("FINAL_REPORT",
    842                                                          $sformatf("Detected faults      = %0d", detected_fault_count),
    843                                                          UVM_NONE)
    844                                              
    845             1                          1             `uvm_info("FINAL_REPORT",
    846                                                          $sformatf("Fault coverage       = %0.2f %%", fault_coverage),
    847                                                          UVM_NONE)
    848                                              
    849             1                          1             `uvm_info("FINAL_REPORT",
    850                                                          $sformatf("Error per fault      = %0.2f", error_per_fault),
    851                                                          UVM_NONE)
    852                                              
    853             1                          1             `uvm_info("PYTHON_RESULT",
    854                                                          $sformatf("MISMATCH=%0d TOTAL_ERROR=%0d MAX_ERROR=%0d INJECTED=%0d DETECTED=%0d",
    855                                                                    mismatch_count,
    856                                                                    total_error,
    857                                                                    max_error,
    858                                                                    injected_fault_count,
    859                                                                    detected_fault_count),
    860                                                          UVM_NONE)
    861                                              
    862             1                          1             `uvm_info("FINAL_REPORT", "=================================================", UVM_NONE)
    863                                              
    864                                                      if (injected_fault_count == 0 && fail_count == 0) begin
    865             1                    ***0***                 `uvm_info("FINAL_REPORT", "NORMAL TEST RESULT: PASSED", UVM_NONE)
    866                                                      end else if (injected_fault_count > 0 && mismatch_count > 0) begin
    867             1                          1                 `uvm_info("FINAL_REPORT", "FAULT TEST RESULT: FAULT EFFECT DETECTED", UVM_NONE)
    868                                                      end else if (injected_fault_count > 0 && mismatch_count == 0) begin
    869             1                    ***0***                 `uvm_warning("FINAL_REPORT", "FAULT TEST RESULT: FAULT MASKED / NOT DETECTED")
    870                                                      end else begin
    871             1                    ***0***                 `uvm_error("FINAL_REPORT", "TEST FAILED")
    872                                                      end
    873                                              
    874             1                          1             `uvm_info("FINAL_REPORT", "=================================================", UVM_NONE)
    875                                                  endfunction

    82                                               
    83                                                           logic [PE_SUM_WIDTH-1:0] clean_pe_word;
    84                                                           logic [PE_SUM_WIDTH-1:0] faulted_pe_word;
    85                                                           logic [PE_SUM_WIDTH-1:0] local_fault_mask;
    86                                               
    87              1                        192                 assign clean_pe_word =
    88                                                               clean_pe_sum_flat[(fp*PE_SUM_WIDTH) +: PE_SUM_WIDTH];
    89                                               
    90              1                        192                 always_comb begin
    91              1                        192                     local_fault_mask = '0;
    92                                               
    93                                                               if (fault_active_reg && (fault_pe_id_reg == fp[5:0])) begin
    94                                                                   if (fault_bit_pos_reg < PE_SUM_WIDTH) begin
    95              1                          1                             local_fault_mask[fault_bit_pos_reg] = 1'b1;
    96                                                                   end
    97                                                               end
    98                                                           end
    99                                               
    100             1                        257                 assign faulted_pe_word = clean_pe_word ^ local_fault_mask;
    101                                              
    102                                                          assign pe_sum_flat[(fp*PE_SUM_WIDTH) +: PE_SUM_WIDTH] =
    103                                                              faulted_pe_word;
    104                                              
    105                                                      end
    106                                                  endgenerate
    107                                              
    108                                                  // ------------------------------------------------------------
    109                                                  // Latched PE valid vector
    110                                                  // raw_pe_valid_flat pulses at different times for different PEs.
    111                                                  // valid_seen_vec remembers which PEs have already produced valid output.
    112                                                  // ------------------------------------------------------------
    113                                                  assign pe_valid_flat = valid_seen_vec;
    114                                              
    115                                                  // ------------------------------------------------------------
    116                                                  // Systolic skewed valid generation
    117                                                  //
    118                                                  // For PE[i][j]:
    119                                                  // activation reaches after j cycles
    120                                                  // weight reaches after i cycles
    121                                                  //
    122                                                  // To make valid_a and valid_b meet at every PE:
    123                                                  // row_valid_in[i] is launched at cycle i
    124                                                  // col_valid_in[j] is launched at cycle j
    125                                                  // ------------------------------------------------------------
    126             1                         25         always_ff @(posedge clk or negedge rst_n) begin
    127                                                      if (!rst_n) begin
    128             1                          2                 running           <= 1'b0;
    129             1                          2                 cycle_count       <= 8'd0;
    130             1                          2                 done              <= 1'b0;
    131             1                          2                 result_valid      <= 1'b0;
    132                                              
    133             1                          2                 row_valid_in      <= '0;
    134             1                          2                 col_valid_in      <= '0;
    135             1                          2                 valid_seen_vec    <= '0;
    136                                              
    137             1                          2                 fault_active_reg  <= 1'b0;
    138             1                          2                 fault_pe_id_reg   <= 6'd0;
    139             1                          2                 fault_bit_pos_reg <= 6'd0;
    140                                                      end else begin
    141             1                         23                 done         <= 1'b0;
    142             1                         23                 result_valid <= 1'b0;
    143                                              
    144                                                          // Latch valid pulses from array.
    145             1                         23                 valid_seen_vec <= valid_seen_vec | raw_pe_valid_flat;
    146                                              
    147                                                          // Latch one-cycle fault request from UVM driver.
    148                                                          if (fault_en) begin
    149             1                          1                     fault_active_reg  <= 1'b1;
    150             1                          1                     fault_pe_id_reg   <= fault_pe_id;
    151             1                          1                     fault_bit_pos_reg <= fault_bit_pos;
    152                                                          end
    153                                              
    154                                                          if (clear_acc) begin
    155             1                          1                     running           <= 1'b0;
    156             1                          1                     cycle_count       <= 8'd0;
    157             1                          1                     row_valid_in      <= '0;
    158             1                          1                     col_valid_in      <= '0;
    159             1                          1                     valid_seen_vec    <= '0;
    160                                              
    161             1                          1                     fault_active_reg  <= 1'b0;
    162             1                          1                     fault_pe_id_reg   <= 6'd0;
    163             1                          1                     fault_bit_pos_reg <= 6'd0;
    164                                                          end else if (start && !running) begin
    165             1                          1                     running      <= 1'b1;
    166             1                          1                     cycle_count  <= 8'd1;
    167                                              
    168             1                          1                     valid_seen_vec <= '0;
    169                                              
    170             1                          1                     row_valid_in <= '0;
    171             1                          1                     col_valid_in <= '0;
    172                                              
    173             1                          1                     row_valid_in[0] <= 1'b1;
    174             1                          1                     col_valid_in[0] <= 1'b1;
    175                                                          end else if (running) begin
    176             1                         16                     cycle_count <= cycle_count + 1'b1;
    177                                              
    178             1                         16                     row_valid_in <= '0;
    179             1                         16                     col_valid_in <= '0;
    180                                              
    181                                                              if (cycle_count < N) begin
    182             1                          7                         row_valid_in[cycle_count] <= 1'b1;
    183             1                          7                         col_valid_in[cycle_count] <= 1'b1;
    184                                                              end
    185                                              
    186                                                              if (&(valid_seen_vec | raw_pe_valid_flat)) begin
    187             1                          1                         running      <= 1'b0;
    188             1                          1                         done         <= 1'b1;
    189             1                          1                         result_valid <= 1'b1;
    190                                                              end else if (cycle_count >= ARRAY_LATENCY[7:0]) begin
    191             1                    ***0***                         running      <= 1'b0;
    192             1                    ***0***                         done         <= 1'b1;
    193             1                    ***0***                         result_valid <= 1'b1;
    194                                                              end
    195                                                          end else begin
    196             1                          5                     row_valid_in <= '0;
    197             1                          5                     col_valid_in <= '0;
    198                                                          end
    199                                                      end
    200                                                  end
    201                                              
    202                                              endmodule
